Let's investigate the LP SOTA method. It might converge to optimum of the continuous/discrete problem. The primal LP for current f: maximize sum b subject f*b ≤ M, b≥0. If f is a probability density, M = max f*f (unnormalized). This is linear! But earlier we thought c_l not convex; but this LP is different. It is used to find a direction g that satisfies (f*g)≤M. Since f and g nonnegative. It is a linear program over g; objective sum g. This is like finding the largest total mass distribution g such that convolution with fixed f is bounded by M. If f* f ≤M and sum f fixed, g=f is feasible. LP optimum will have sum g ≥ sum f (since f feasible). Then rescale to same sum and convex combine? For f with score S=2nM/(sum f)^2. If sum g > sum f, rescale g down? Wait they maximize sum b subject f*b≤M. Since f feasible with sum f. Rescale g to same normalization: sum g becomes same, but (f*g_norm)≤ (sum f/sum b) M ≤M. ```
import numpy as np, time, math
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except: linprog=None

def as_finite_array(x): ...

def conv_score(x, normal=None): # no clipping? assumes [0,1000]
    n=x.size
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    c=np.convolve(x,x, mode='full')
    return 2*n*c.max()/(s*s)
```

LP solve function:
```
def solve_lp_b(f, M, time_limit=None):
    n=f.size
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
    # fill rows
    for l in range(n): A[l,:l+1]=f[l::-1]
    for l in range(n,2*n-1): A[l,l-n+1:]=f[n-1:l-n]
    res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':time_limit, 'presolve': True})
    if res.success or res.status in (0,1?) and res.x finite: b=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
    else: b=f.copy()
    # ensure not too large? If b sum huge due solver, rescale? Rescale g to S0; if b all zero fallback f.
    return b
```
If linprog unavailable, use a heuristic for g: We can solve LP approximately by greedy? Maybe. But scipy available.

def smooth_lse_gradient(c, beta):
    c_max = c.max()
    expc = np.exp(beta*(c-c_max))
    w = expc/expc.sum()
    # gradient terms for non-diagonal:
    p = f
    rp = p[::-1]
    conv = np.convolve(w, rp, mode='full')  # length (2n-1)+(n-1)=3n-2? Wait w length 2n-1, p length n, conv full length 3n-2. Need slice [n-1 : 2n-2?]. Let's verify.
```

Given time, we should implement a robust numerical optimizer that can find the global/local low point. The most promising are:
- Full mirror descent / smoothed max from good initializations (including inverse-root).
- LP-based subproblem with tie-breaking/mixing and multiple restarts.
- Direct low-dimensional perturbations (add smooth components) to inverse-root.
Could combine all within budget.

Let's design final code with stages:
Stage 0: constants, helpers.
Stage 1: choose lengths. We can run for N = parent length if available and maybe N=1024/2048? The best final could be any N. Running multiple N: optimized sequences for N=512 and N=3809. For N=3809, LP impossible; but mirror can run many iterations. We can use parent length. For N=1024, even faster; can run LP. Maybe best to optimize both.

Stage 2: Initializations for each N:
- `init_uniform(N)`: ones * S0/N.
- `init_power(N, beta, shift)`: raw=(i+shift)^(-beta) normalized to S0.
- `init_poly(N, a)`: raw=(i/N + shift)^a? Better use `raw=(x)**a` normalized where x=(i+0.5)/N.
- `init_exp(N, a)`: raw=exp(a*x).
- `init_beta(N, aa, bb)`: raw=(i+0.5)^aa*(N-i)^bb? Use via logs for negative exponents.
- `init_parent_resized(N)`: if parent available, area resample to N and normalize to S0.
- `init_random(N, rng, K=8)`: generate log weights as sum of sines with random coefficients, normalize. Also maybe random smoothed Gaussian.
Evaluate exact internal score M/S0? Actually if normalized S0, score=M. Use `norm_to_sum(raw,S0)` and `score_M(x) = max(np.convolve(x,x))` since sumS0. Ensure x<=1000. No cap issues.

Stage 3: Smoothed max optimization (mirror descent) on chosen N. For each initial among top few, run for a time slice.

Implement `smooth_step(f, beta, rng, deadline)`:
- Compute c = full conv f. M=c.max(). If M not finite return.
- Weights w = exp(beta*(c - M))/sum. To avoid underflow, if beta*M >700 scale. Use `z = beta*(c - M)`, W=np.exp(z), W/=W.sum()`.
- Compute G:
```
p=f
rp=p[::-1]
convw = np.convolve(W, rp, 'full')
A = convw[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n? Let's test length: W len2n-1, rp len n, conv len3n-2. slice n-1 to 2n-1 length n? 2n-1 - (n-1)=n. Yes.
diag = 0.0
# only even l: i even? l=2i; w index 2i.
G = A.copy()
idx = np.arange(0,n)
lidx = 2*idx
G += W[lidx] * p  # if lidx < len W (true max2n-2)
```
But A formula includes all w_l p_{l-i}. For l even and 2i, includes p_i; adding W[lidx]*p_i makes 2. Good. For l odd none.
- Normalize G? `delta = p * G` for log-gradient. Subtract mean to preserve: `delta -= delta.mean()`. Maybe apply low-pass: `delta = np.convolve(delta, np.ones(smooth)/smooth, 'same')` and re-subtract mean. Smooth factor maybe int(sqrt(n)/4) to avoid noise. But too much slows. Use optional if high beta.
- Try step sizes:
```
base_eta = 0.05 / max(1.0, np.std(delta)+1e-12)
etas = np.array([0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0])*base_eta
best = None; bestM=M
for e in etas:
    u = -e*delta
    u = np.clip(u, -5, 5) # to avoid huge
    cand = p * np.exp(u)
    cand = clip0 renorm to S0
    M2 = max conv cand
    if M2 < bestM: bestM=M2,best=cand
```
Maybe because subtract mean, for linear updates sum not preserved but normalize. Use clip u to [-3,3]. Return if best found else maybe small random.
- Additionally, if no improvement for a while, increase beta and/or add random perturbation.

But the gradient of logsumexp may point to lower average c, not necessarily max. The update with `delta = p*G - mean` should reduce weighted sum; line search ensures M improves. Good.

Potential issue with W concentrated on a single coefficient: gradient G is shifted p; delta entropy may be stable.

Stage 4: LP refinement for smaller N (e.g. N=1024) using the best of initial/smooth.
Implement `lp_direction(f, M, ...)` as primal. But from inverse-root, LP may provide direction. We can call a few times. Use line search and mixing.

LP code:
```
def lp_solve(f, M, S0, remaining):
    n=f.size
    if not linprog: return f.copy()
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for l in range(n): A[l,:l+1]=f[l::-1]
    for l in range(n,2*n-1): A[l,l-n+1:]=f[n-1:l-n]
    # constraints A b <= M
    cobj=-np.ones(n)
    # to encourage alignment, maybe use two-stage: solve max sum, then max dot(f,b) with sum>=S0? But linprog only one. We can combine with tiny epsilon: cobj = -ones + eps*(-f/S0)? Actually maximizing sum + eps dot(f,b) = minimize -sum - eps dot(f,S0?).
    eps = 1e-7 / max(1.0, M)
    cobj = -np.ones(n) - eps * S0 * f? Units: f sum S0, f_i ~0.02, eps*S0*f ~eps? Need not.
    res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': min(30, remaining*0.2)})
    b=...
```
If one-stage with tiny eps, objective may sacrifice sum slightly, but if sacrifice <tol safe. We can then rescale g to S0: if sum_b >= S0*(1-1e-6), safe if factor<=1; else if sum_b <S0, we can either discard or mix with f and rescale only if f*b≤M? Safer: if sum_b <S0*0.999, we can blend b with f: `gtmp = f.copy(); if b sum>0: gtmp = (b/S0?)?` Need maintain sum≥S0. We can just set b = f.copy() on failure.
- If successful, mix with f: `mix_alpha = 0.5 + 0.5*rng.random()`? To smooth, use `g = (1-mix)*f + mix*b` where b normalized? Ensure `g` sum maybe. But if b sum significantly >S0, `b` has sum >S0, mixing with f sum S0 yields sum >S0; rescale down safe. Let `b_norm = b * (S0/b.sum())` to sum S0. Then `g = (1-mix)*f + mix*b_norm`, sum S0, and f*g? Since b_norm=f? f*b_norm may violate M if mix? We know f*b≤M. If b_norm = α b with α≤1 (if b sum≥S0), f*b_norm≤α M≤M. f*f≤M. Convex combo f*g≤M. Safe. If b sum >S0, α<1, safe. If b sum=S0, safe. Good. So normalize b to S0 even if sum>S0; if sum<S0, α>1 violates. In that case blend with f such that total sum≥S0? If b sum< S0, t such that (1-t)S0+t sum_b =S0? impossible unless t=0. We can set b=f if sum<S0.
- Line search λ on `cand=(1-λ)f+λ g` (no need rescale). Evaluate M, pick best. Because g safe f*g≤M, derivative nonpositive but may still worsen due g*g. Use λ grid.

LP can be run for N=1024/2048. But if we optimize full N=3809 with mirror, maybe enough.

Stage 5: Local coordinate/patch refinement:

def _clip_rescale(x, S):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1000.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-12:
       return None
    return x*(S/s)

LP line search:
```
def lp_step(f, M, S0, deadline, mix=None):
    A=build_conv_matrix(f)
    Mvec=np.full(A.shape[0], M)
    # objective maximize sum + tiny alignment:
    eps = 1e-8
    cobj = -np.ones(n) - eps * (f / max(f.max(),1e-12)) maybe.
    res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mvec, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    b=res.x; clip
    sb=b.sum()
    if sb < S0*0.999: b=f.copy(); sb=S0
    b = b*S0/sb
    g = (1-mix)*f + mix*b
    # line search λ
    best=f, bestM=M
    for lam in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
       cand=(1-lam)*f + lam*g
       cand_norm = normalize to S0 (if clipping)
       M2=conv_max
       if M2<bestM: ...
    return best,bestM
```

import numpy as np, time, math
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except: linprog=None

# helpers: sanitize, score, convmax, normalize, raised cosine bump

def _score(x):
    if x is None or len==0: return inf
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    return 2*len(x)*np.convolve(x,x,'full').max()/(s*s)

def _make_raw(raw,N): ...

def _resample_to_length(arr,N): ...

def _raised_cosine(center,width,N): ...

def _smooth_initializations(N,rng,parent): returns list

Helper `_optimize_smooth`:
Detailed implement:
```
def _optimize_smooth(f, deadline, rng, min_improve=1e-8, max_iter=5000):
    f=np.asarray(f,float).copy()
    N=f.size; S=f.sum()
    best=f.copy(); best_sc=_score(best)
    beta=20.0
    no_improve=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=np.convolve(f,f,'full')
       M=c.max()
       sc=2*N*M/(f.sum()**2)
       if sc < best_sc: best=f.copy(); best_sc=sc
       # if M too large? no
       z=beta*(c-M)
       # Underflow safe: z<=0; min maybe -inf; exp underflows okay.
       W=np.exp(z)
       sW=W.sum()
       if not finite or sW==0: W=np.zeros_like(c); active = c>=M-1e-12; W[active]=1; sW=W.sum()
       W/=sW
       rp=f[::-1]
       convW=np.convolve(W, rp, 'full')
       A=convW[N-1:2*N-1]
       G=A + W[0:2*N-1:2]*f  # W[2i]
       delta = f*G
       delta -= delta.mean()
       # low-pass
       if N>2:
          sm=max(1,int(math.sqrt(N)//4))
          if sm>1:
             kernel=np.ones(sm)/sm
             delta=np.convolve(delta,kernel,'same')
             delta-=delta.mean()
       scale=np.std(delta)
       if scale<1e-12:
           # random small perturb
           delta=rng.normal(0,1,N); delta-=delta.mean()
       etas = np.array([1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2])* (1.0/scale)
       # Also if M small? no
       chosen=False
       for e in etas:
           if e*scale>50: u=-50? better use u=-e*delta; clip.
           u=-e*delta
           u=np.clip(u,-4,4)
           cand=f*np.exp(u)
           cand_s=cand.sum()
           if cand_s<=0 or not finite: continue
           cand=cand*(f.sum()/cand_s)
           if cand.max()>1000: cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cand=cand*(f.sum()/cand.sum())
           c2=np.convolve(cand,cand,'full').max()
           sc2=2*N*c2/(cand.sum()**2)
           if sc2 < best_sc*(1-1e-12) or sc2 < sc*(1-1e-9):
               if sc2 < best_sc: best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc2
               f=cand; chosen=True; break
       if chosen:
           beta = min(80.0, beta*1.03)
           no_improve=0
       else:
           beta = min(200.0, beta*1.15)
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve>=6:
               # perturbation from best
               f=best.copy()
               # add random smooth log noise
               k= max(1,N//200)
               for _ in range(10): add prior? Simpler:
               idx=rng.integers(0,N)
               width=rng.integers(1,max(2,N//50))
               amp=rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)*f.sum()
               bump=_raised_cosine bounded*amp
               f=f+bump; normalize; if no improve, best
               beta=30.0; no_improve=0
       iter+=1
    return best,best_sc
```
But line search uses `best_sc` update; if candidate improves current but not global? It will be chosen if sc2<sc. Good. If no candidate improves, perturb.

Potential issue: `f.sum()` after multiplicative update might be S0; we use ratio to preserve current sum. But the objective scale invariant; preserving S0 okay. However if f sum not exactly S0 after perturb, use current sum.

Step etas scaling: `1.0/scale` could be huge if delta std small. Clip u to [-4,4] ensures relative change factor e^±4 ~55, too big? But line search exact prevents. Smaller u maybe. Use max abs 3. Maybe spiky. Use u clip [-2,2] for safety. If scale small, all updates small; okay.

The low-pass smoothing on delta each iter can be costly O(n) but fine. It may remove small-scale improvements. Use sm small: `max(1,int(N**0.25))` (~8 for 3809). Good.

Potential underflow in `W=np.exp(beta*(c-M))`: If beta up to200 and c-M min -0.5 => exp(-100) underflows zero; W concentrated on active few. That's okay. But if all active equal and beta high, many equal exp0; sum large but fine. Use `z=beta*(c-M)-np.log(1e-300)?` no. `np.exp` underflow to0 safe. If `sW==0`, fallback to active near max.

Could choose beta adaptation: If many active (W.sum after adding small), low beta; if one, high. Start beta=10. Increase slowly. For exact max, high beta needed. But line search only evaluates true M, so beta path matters. Maybe set beta dynamically based on gap: `gap = np.percentile(c,99)-M`? If gap small, high beta; else moderate. Simpler: beta = min(200, max(5, 20 / max(1e-6, gap?))). Let's not overcomplicate; adaptive above okay.

We need ensure the `convW` slice length exactly N. Let's test N=1: convW length W len1 + rp len1 -1 =1; slice N-1=0 to 2N-1=2? Python slice 0:2 but length1, okay? It will return length1? Actually slice end greater than len returns len. fine. But N large. For N=1, W[0:2N-1:2]=W[0:1:2] len1. Good.

But A computation for N>1: `convW[N-1:2*N-1]` end exclusive 2N-1, length N? For N=4, convW length (7+4-1)=10, indices 3,4,5,6 length4. Good.

Now LP stage implementation:
```
def _build_conv_A(f,N):
    A=np.zeros((2*N-1,N),dtype=np.float64)
    for l in range(N):
       A[l,:l+1]=f[l::-1]
    for l in range(N,2*N-1):
       A[l,l-N+1:]=f[N-1:l-N]
    return A
```
For N=1024, loops okay. Could maybe build via scipy? fine.

LP step with mix options:
```
def _lp_improve(f, deadline, rng, max_iters=200):
    N=f.size; S=f.sum()
    best=f.copy(); best_sc=_score(best)
    for mix in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3]: # maybe loop within iters?
       no=0
       while time < deadline:
          M=...; sc=...
          if sc<best_sc: update
          A=build
          cobj=-np.ones(N)
          # add regex: maximize dot(f,b) tiny
          eps=1e-9
          cobj = cobj - eps * f / max(f.max(),1e-12)
          if linprog is None: break
          time_limit = max(1.0, min(20.0, (deadline-time)*0.2))
          res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*N-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
          if res is not None and res.x is not None:
             b=np.asarray(res.x,float)
             b=np.clip(b,0,None)
             sb=b.sum()
             if sb >= S*0.999 and np.isfinite(sb):
                b=b*S/sb
             else:
                b=f.copy()
          else:
             b=f.copy(); no+=1; if no>5 break; continue
          g=(1-mix)*f + mix*b
          # line search on λ
          chosen=False
          for lam in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
              cand=(1-lam)*f+lam*g
              cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cs=cand.sum(); if cs<=0: continue
              cand=cand*S/cs
              M2=convmax(cand); sc2=2*N*M2/S^2
              if sc2<best_sc: best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc2
              if sc2<sc:
                  f=cand; chosen=True; break
          if not chosen:
             no+=1
          else: no=0
          if no>10: break
    return best,best_sc
```

def _patch_search(f, deadline, rng):
    N=f.size; S=f.sum(); best=f.copy(); best_sc=_score(best)
    while time<deadline:
       # choose bump
       width = max(1,int(rng.integers(max(1,N//2000), N//20)))
       center = int(rng.integers(-width//2, N+width//2))
       shape = _bump_shape(center,width,N)
       amp = rng.uniform(0.001,0.05)*S
       if rng.random()<0.4: amp=-amp*0.5
       cand=f+amp*shape
       cand=normalize; sc=_score(cand)
       if sc<best_sc*(1-1e-10): best=cand; f=cand? maybe accept if improves current with Metropolis? Use best for next.
    return best
```

The random smooth generator:
```
def _random_smooth_N(N,rng,scale=1.0, singular=False):
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    K=12
    coeffs = rng.normal(0, 1.0/ (np.arange(K)+1)**0.7, K)
    phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,K)
    w=0
    for k in range(K):
       w += coeffs[k]*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + phase[k])
    raw=np.exp(w*scale)
    if singular:
       raw *= (x+0.01)**-0.3
    # also random endpoint bias:
    raw *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3)* x)
    return raw
```
Can produce good.

```
for aa in [-0.5,-0.3,0,0.3]:
 for bb in [0,0.3,0.5,1.0]:
   raw=np.exp(aa*np.log(i+0.5)+bb*np.log(N-i+0.5) + rng.normal(0,0.2)* (i/N))
```

def _make_inits(N,rng,parent):

   def add_raw(raw):

      raw=np.clip(raw,0,None); s=raw.sum(); if s<=0 return
      v=raw*S/s; v=np.clip(v,0,1000); v=v*S/v.sum(); if v.size and finite: inits.append(v)

   add_raw((i+0.5)**-0.5)

```
def _theta_to_vec(theta,N):
    alpha=float(np.clip(theta[0], -1.0, 1.5))
    beta=float(np.clip(theta[1], -1.0, 1.5))
    tilt=float(theta[2])
    K=(len(theta)-3)//2
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    left=(np.arange(N)+1.0)**alpha
    right=(N - np.arange(N) + 1.0)**beta  # min 1 at last if shift1; for singular -0.5 sum harmonic? Actually (N-i+1) at i=0 N, last1.
    logw=tilt*x
    idx=np.arange(3,len(theta))
    for k in range(K):
       a=theta[idx[2*k]]; b=theta[idx[2*k+1]]
       ang=2*np.pi*k*x
       logw = logw + a*np.cos(ang) + b*np.sin(ang)
    logw=np.clip(logw, -50,50)
    raw=left*right*np.exp(logw)
    raw=np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.0, posinf=1e300, neginf=0.0)
    return _make_from_raw(raw,S)
```

`_safe_clip_normalize(raw, S)`:
```
def _raw_to_vec(raw,N,S=None):
    if S is None: S=math.sqrt(2*N)
    raw=np.asarray(raw,dtype=np.float64)
    raw=raw.ravel()
    raw=np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
    raw=raw.clip(0.0, None)
    s=raw.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    v=raw*(S/s)
    vmax=v.max()
    if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax<=0: return None
    if vmax > 1000.0:
        v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0)
        s=v.sum()
        if s<=0: return None
        v=v*(S/s)
        if v.max()>1000.0:
            v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return None
    return v
```
For internal M score, if v sum not S due to clip, use `_score` exact.

`_score`: For speed and exact objective without evaluating list each time:
```
def _score_vec(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0).clip(0.0,1000.0)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    c=np.convolve(x,x,mode='full')
    m=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
This mimics evaluator after clipping. For internal normalized sum ~S, okay. Avoid `tolist` overhead.

for k in range(1,K+1):

```
def _fourier_search(N, deadline, rng, theta0=None, K=8):
    if minimize is None or deadline - time.monotonic() < 20: return None,None
    c0=np.log(np.arange(N)+1.0)
    cr=np.log(N-np.arange(N)+1.0)
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    coslist=[]; sinlist=[]
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2*pi*k*x; coslist.append(np.cos(ang)); sinlist.append(np.sin(ang))
    def build(th):
       alpha=th[0]; beta=th[1]; tilt=th[2]
       logw = alpha*c0 + beta*cr + tilt*x
       idx=3
       for k in range(K):
           logw = logw + th[idx]*coslist[k] + th[idx+1]*sinlist[k]
           idx+=2
       logw=np.clip(logw,-50,50)
       raw=np.exp(logw)
       return _raw_to_vec(raw,N)
    def soft(th):
       v=build(th)
       if v is None: return 1e6
       c=np.convolve(v,v,'full'); m=c.max()
       z=60.0*(c-m)
       val=m + math.log(float(np.exp(z).sum()))/60.0
       if not finite: return 1e6
       return float(val)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None


def _raw_to_vec(raw, N, S=None):
    if raw is None:
        return None
    raw = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
    raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, None)
    if raw.size != N:
        raw = raw[:N] if raw.size >= N else np.pad(raw, (0, max(0, N - raw.size)))
    s = float(raw.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    if S is None:
        S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(N))
    v = raw * (S / s)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)) or float(v.max()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    if float(v.max()) > 1000.0:
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sv = float(v.sum())
        if sv <= 1e-300:
            return None
        v = v * (S / sv)
        if float(v.max()) > 1000.0:
            v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return None
    return v


def _score_vec(x):
    if x is None:
        return float("inf")
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(x.size)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _resample_vec(parent, N):
    try:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 1e-300:
        return None
    cum = np.cumsum(p) / ps
    old_grid = (np.arange(p.size) + 0.5) / float(p.size)
    new_grid = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) / float(N)
    new_cum = np.interp(new_grid, old_grid, cum)
    mass = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0.0], new_cum)))
    mass = np.clip(mass, 0.0, None)
    return _raw_to_vec(mass, N)


def _theta_to_vec(theta, N):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size < 3:
        return None
    K = max(0, (theta.size - 3) // 2)
    alpha = float(np.clip(theta[0], -1.0, 1.0))
    beta = float(np.clip(theta[1], -1.0, 1.0))
    tilt = float(theta[2])
    i = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64)
    left = (i + 1.0) ** alpha
    right = (N - i + 1.0)
    right = np.where(right > 0.0, right, 1.0)
    beta_log = np.log(np.where(right > 0.0, right, 1.0))
    logw = alpha * np.log(i + 1.0) + beta * beta_log
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(N)
    logw += tilt * x
    for k in range(K):
        a = float(theta[3 + 2 * k])
        b = float(theta[3 + 2 * k + 1])
        ang = 2.0 * math.pi * (k + 1) * x
        logw = logw + a * np.cos(ang) + b * np.sin(ang)
    raw = np.exp(np.clip(logw, -50.0, 50.0))
    return _raw_to_vec(raw, N)


def _pad_theta(theta, K):
    target = 3 + 2 * K
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if th.size == target:
        return th
    out = np.zeros(target, dtype=np.float64)
    m = min(target, th.size)
    if m > 0:
        out[:m] = th[:m]
    out[0] = float(np.clip(out[0], -1.0, 1.0))
    out[1] = float(np.clip(out[1], -1.0, 1.0))
    return out


def _fourier_search(N, deadline, rng, theta0=None, K=8):
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if minimize is None:
        return None, None
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25.0:
        return None, None

    i = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(N)
    log_left = np.log(i + 1.0)
    right = np.where((N - i + 1.0) > 0.0, (N - i + 1.0), 1.0)
    log_right = np.log(right)
    cos_list, sin_list = [], []
    for k in range(K):
        ang = 2.0 * math.pi * (k + 1) * x
        cos_list.append(np.cos(ang))
        sin_list.append(np.sin(ang))

    def build(th):
        th = np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if th.size < 3:
            return None
        alpha = float(np.clip(th[0], -1.0, 1.0))
        beta = float(np.clip(th[1], -1.0, 1.0))
        tilt = float(th[2])
        logw = alpha * log_left + beta * log_right + tilt * x
        idx = 3
        for k in range(K):
            if idx + 1 >= th.size:
                break
            logw = logw + th[idx] * cos_list[k] + th[idx + 1] * sin_list[k]
            idx += 2
        raw = np.exp(np.clip(logw, -50.0, 50.0))
        return _raw_to_vec(raw, N)

    def soft_obj(th):
        v = build(th)
        if v is None:
            return 1e6
        c = np.convolve(v, v, mode="full")
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return 1e6
        beta_soft = 60.0
        z = beta_soft * (c - m)
        s = float(np.exp(z).sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return 1e6
        return float(m + math.log(s) / beta_soft)

    bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0), (-1.0, 1.0), (-3.0, 3.0)]
    bounds += [(-2.0, 2.0)] * (2 * K)

    zeros = [0.0] * (2 * K)
    starts = [
        np.array([-0.5, 0.0, 0.0] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([-0.5, 0.0, 0.6] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([-0.5, 0.0, -0.6] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([-0.4, 0.0, 0.0] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([-0.5, -0.25, 0.0] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([0.0, 0.0, 0.0] + zeros, dtype=np.float64),
    ]
    if theta0 is not None:
        starts.insert(0, _pad_theta(theta0, K))
    for _ in range(4):
        th = np.empty(3 + 2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
        th[0] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        th[1] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        th[2] = rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)
        th[3:] = rng.normal(0.7, 1.0)
        starts.append(th)

    best_vec = None
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_theta = None

    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        v = build(st)
        if v is not None:
            sc = _score_vec(v)
            if sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc
                best_vec = v.copy()
                best_theta = st.copy()

        maxfev = 900 if N <= 768 else 520
        try:
            res = minimize(
                soft_obj,
                st,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": 250, "maxfev": maxfev, "ftol": 1e-10, "gtol": 1e-6},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            v2 = build(res.x)
            if v2 is not None:
                sc2 = _score_vec(v2)
                if sc2 < best_exact:
                    best_exact = sc2
                    best_vec = v2.copy()
                    best_theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

    return best_vec, best_theta


def _make_inits(N, rng, parent, best_theta=None):
    inits = []
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(N))
    i = np.arange(N)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(N)

    def add_vec(v):
        if v is None:
            return
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        if v.size != N or not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            return
        sc = _score_vec(v)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            inits.append((sc, v))

    def add_raw(raw):
        add_vec(_raw_to_vec(raw, N, S))

    add_raw(np.ones(N))
    if parent is not None:
        add_vec(_resample_vec(parent, N))

    add_raw((i + 1.0) ** -0.5)
    for sh in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0):
        add_raw((i + sh) ** -0.5)

    for b in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.0):
        if b == 0.0:
            add_raw(np.ones(N))
        else:
            add_raw((i + 1.0) ** (-b))

    for a in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        add_raw(np.exp(a * x))

    for aa in (-0.5, -0.3, -0.1, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
        for bb in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
            raw = np.exp(aa * np.log(i + 1.0) + bb * np.log(N - i + 1.0))
            add_raw(raw)

    K4 = 8
    if best_theta is not None:
        add_vec(_theta_to_vec(_pad_theta(best_theta, K4), N))

    det_thetas = [
        [-0.5, 0.0, 0.0],
        [-0.5, 0.0, 0.35],
        [-0.5, -0.2, 0.0],
        [-0.4, 0.0, -0.25],
        [0.0, 0.0, 0.4],
    ]
    for base in det_thetas:
        add_vec(_theta_to_vec(base + [0.0] * (2 * K4), N))

    for _ in range(14):
        K = 9
        coefs = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        coefs /= (np.arange(K) + 1.0) ** 0.45
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, K)
        w = 0.0
        scale = rng.uniform(0.7, 1.8)
        for k in range(K):
            w += scale * coefs[k] * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * (k + 1) * x + phases[k])
        raw = np.exp(np.clip(w, -6.0, 6.0))
        if rng.random() < 0.55:
            raw = raw * (i + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.05))
        if rng.random() < 0.45:
            raw = raw * (N - i + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.05))
        add_raw(raw)

    inits.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    out = []
    last_sc = None
    for sc, v in inits[:50]:
        if last_sc is None or abs(sc - last_sc) > 1e-4 * max(1.0, last_sc):
            out.append(v)
            last_sc = sc
        if len(out) >= 6:
            break
    if not out:
        out.append(_raw_to_vec((i + 1.0) ** -0.5, N, S))
    return out


def _bump_shape(N, rng):
    width = int(rng.integers(max(2, N // 2500), max(3, N // 12)))
    center = int(rng.integers(-width // 2, N + width // 2))
    left = max(0, center - width)
    right = min(N, center + width + 1)
    if right <= left:
        return None
    xs = np.arange(left, right, dtype=np.float64)
    shape = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    sig = max(1.0, float(width) / 2.0)
    vals = np.exp(-((xs - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    shape[left:right] = vals
    s = float(shape.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return shape / s


def _patch_search(f0, deadline, rng):
    f = np.asarray(f0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    S = float(f.sum())
    N = int(f.size)
    if f is None or N == 0 or S <= 0.0:
        return f0
    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = _score_vec(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return f0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            if rng.random() < 0.75:
                shape = _bump_shape(N, rng)
                if shape is None:
                    continue
                amp = rng.uniform(0.002, 0.06) * S
                if rng.random() < 0.38:
                    amp = -0.65 * amp
                cand = f + amp * shape
            else:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, N)
                k = max(1, int(np.sqrt(N) // 5))
                if k > 1:
                    ker = np.ones(k) / k
                    noise = np.convolve(noise, ker, mode="same")
                noise -= float(noise.mean())
                cand = f * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -2.0, 2.0))
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = cand * (S / cs)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = cand * (S / cs)
            sc = _score_vec(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                best = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                f = cand.copy()
        except Exception:
            break

    return best


def _optimize_smooth(f0, deadline, rng):
    f = np.asarray(f0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    N = int(f.size)
    if N <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(f)):
        return f0
    S = float(f.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return f0

    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = _score_vec(best)
    beta = 25.0
    no_improve = 0
    iter_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            c = np.convolve(f, f, mode="full")
            M = float(np.max(c))
            cur_sc = float(2.0 * N * M / (float(f.sum()) ** 2))
            if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc:
                best = f.copy()
                best_sc = cur_sc

            z = beta * (c - M)
            W = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
            sw = float(W.sum())
            if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                near = c >= (M - 1e-10 * max(1.0, M))
                W = np.zeros_like(c)
                W[near] = 1.0
                sw = float(W.sum())
                if sw <= 0.0:
                    W = np.ones_like(c) / float(c.size)
                    sw = 1.0
            W = W / sw

            rv = f[::-1]
            convW = np.convolve(W, rv, mode="full")
            A = convW[N - 1:2 * N - 1].copy()
            G = 2.0 * A
            if A.size != N:
                break

            delta = f * G
            delta = delta - float(delta.mean())

            if N > 8:
                sm = max(1, int(N ** 0.25))
                if sm > 1:
                    ker = np.ones(sm) / float(sm)
                    delta = np.convolve(delta, ker, mode="same")
                    delta = delta - float(delta.mean())

            std = float(np.std(delta))
            if std <= 1e-12:
                delta = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, N)
                delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
                std = float(np.std(delta))
                if std <= 1e-12:
                    std = 1.0

            base = 1.0 / max(1e-12, std)
            multipliers = (1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2)
            chosen = False
            for mult in multipliers:
                e = mult * base
                u = -e * delta
                u = np.clip(u, -3.0, 3.0)
                cand = f * np.exp(u)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                    continue
                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if cs <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cs0 = float(f.sum())
                cand = cand * (cs0 / cs)
                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if cs <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = cand * (cs0 / cs)
                sc2 = _score_vec(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < cur_sc * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                    if sc2 < best_sc * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_sc = sc2
                    f = cand.copy()
                    chosen = True
                    break

            if chosen:
                no_improve = 0
                beta = min(120.0, beta * 1.04)
            else:
                beta = min(250.0, beta * 1.18)
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 8:
                    f = best.copy()
                    shape = _bump_shape(N, rng)
                    if shape is not None:
                        amp = rng.uniform(0.005, 0.04) * float(f.sum())
                        if rng.random() < 0.35:
                            amp = -0.5 * amp
                        f = f + amp * shape
                        f = np.clip(f, 0.0, 1000.0)
                        sf = float(f.sum())
                        if sf > 0.0:
                            f = f * (float(best.sum()) / sf)
                            f = np.clip(f, 0.0, 1000.0)
                    beta = 40.0
                    no_improve = 0
            iter_count += 1
            if iter_count > 200000:
                break
        except Exception:
            f = best.copy()
            beta = 30.0
            no_improve = 0

    return best


def _build_conv_A(f, N):
    A = np.zeros((2 * N - 1, N), dtype=np.float64)
    for l in range(N):
        A[l, : l + 1] = f[l::-1]
    for l in range(N, 2 * N - 1):
        start = l - N + 1
        A[l, start:] = f[N - 1:l - N]
    return A


def _lp_search(f0, deadline, rng):
    f = np.asarray(f0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    N = int(f.size)
    if f is None or N <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(f)):
        return f0
    if linprog is None:
        return f0

    S = float(f.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return f0
    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = _score_vec(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 2.0

    solve_fail = 0
    mix = 0.5

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        try:
            c = np.convolve(f, f, mode="full")
            M = float(np.max(c))
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                break
            cur_sc = _score_vec(f)
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                best = f.copy()
                best_sc = cur_sc

            A = _build_conv_A(f, N)
            cobj = -np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
            tl = max(1.0, min(18.0, 0.15 * max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
            res = None
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    cobj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=np.full(2 * N - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
                    bounds=(0.0, None),
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(tl)},
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            b = None
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                bx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if bx.size == N and np.all(np.isfinite(bx)):
                    bx = np.clip(bx, 0.0, None)
                    sb = float(bx.sum())
                    if sb >= 0.999 * S and np.isfinite(sb):
                        b = bx * (S / sb)
                    else:
                        solve_fail += 1
            if b is None:
                solve_fail += 1
                if solve_fail >= 6:
                    break
                f = best.copy()
                mix = 0.6
                continue
            solve_fail = 0

            g = (1.0 - mix) * f + mix * b
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
            gs = float(g.sum())
            if gs <= 1e-12:
                mix = max(0.3, mix / 2.0)
                continue
            g = g * (S / gs)

            chosen = False
            for lam in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.45, 0.3, 0.18, 0.08, 0.03):
                cand = (1.0 - lam) * f + lam * g
                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if cs <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = cand * (S / cs)
                sc2 = _score_vec(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    if sc2 < best_sc:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_sc = sc2
                    if sc2 < cur_sc * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                        f = cand.copy()
                        chosen = True
                        break
            if not chosen:
                mix = max(0.3, mix * 0.75)
                no = solve_fail
                if no >= 10:
                    break
        except Exception:
            f = best.copy()
            break

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1300.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.5, total_budget - 4.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    parent = None
    try:
        parr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parr.ndim != 1:
            parr = parr.ravel()
        parr = np.where(np.isfinite(parr), parr, 0.0)
        parr = np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if parr.size > 0 and float(parr.sum()) > 0.0:
            parent = parr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        N_large = int(min(parent.size, 5000))
    else:
        N_large = 3809
    N_large = max(64, N_large)

    best_vec = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def update_candidate(v):
        nonlocal best_vec, best_score
        if v is None:
            return
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v.size == 0:
            return
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sc = _score_vec(v)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_vec = v.copy()

    if parent is not None:
        update_candidate(_resample_vec(parent, N_large))
    update_candidate(_raw_to_vec((np.arange(N_large) + 1.0) ** -0.5, N_large))

    stage1_end = t0 + 0.14 * max(1.0, hard_deadline - t0)
    if time.monotonic() < stage1_end - 8.0:
        vmed, thmed = _fourier_search(512, stage1_end, rng, None, K=8)
        update_candidate(vmed)
    else:
        thmed = np.array([-0.5, 0.0, 0.0] + [0.0] * 16, dtype=np.float64)

    stage2_end = t0 + 0.30 * max(1.0, hard_deadline - t0)
    if time.monotonic() < stage2_end - 10.0:
        vfine, thfine = _fourier_search(1024, stage2_end, rng, thmed, K=8)
        update_candidate(vfine)
        if thfine is not None:
            thmed = thfine
    update_candidate(_theta_to_vec(thmed, N_large))

    init_list = _make_inits(N_large, rng, parent, thmed)
    for v in init_list:
        update_candidate(v)

    smooth_end = t0 + 0.68 * max(1.0, hard_deadline - t0)
    if time.monotonic() < smooth_end - 10.0:
        stage_start = time.monotonic()
        remaining = init_list[:5]
        count = max(1, len(remaining))
        for idx, f in enumerate(remaining):
            sub_end = stage_start + (smooth_end - stage_start) * (idx + 1) / float(count)
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            if sub_end <= time.monotonic():
                continue
            fv = f.copy()
            fv = _optimize_smooth(fv, min(sub_end, hard_deadline - 15.0), rng)
            update_candidate(fv)

    if best_vec is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 30.0:
        patch_end = t0 + 0.82 * max(1.0, hard_deadline - t0)
        best_vec = _patch_search(best_vec, min(patch_end, hard_deadline - 20.0), rng)
        update_candidate(best_vec)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 45.0:
        N_lp = 1024 if (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 180.0 else 768
        lp_candidates = []
        if best_vec is not None:
            if best_vec.size == N_lp:
                lp_candidates.append(best_vec.copy())
            else:
                lp_candidates.append(_resample_vec(best_vec, N_lp))
        lp_candidates.append(_theta_to_vec(thmed, N_lp))
        lp_candidates.append(_raw_to_vec((np.arange(N_lp) + 1.0) ** -0.5, N_lp))
        lp_candidates = [x for x in lp_candidates if x is not None]
        if lp_candidates:
            lp_candidates.sort(key=lambda z: _score_vec(z))
            lp_start = lp_candidates[0]
            lp_end = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 25.0)
            lp_out = _lp_search(lp_start, lp_end, rng)
            update_candidate(lp_out)

    if best_vec is None:
        best_vec = _raw_to_vec((np.arange(256) + 1.0) ** -0.5, 256)
    if best_vec is None:
        best_vec = _raw_to_vec(np.ones(128), 128)

    final_vec = np.asarray(best_vec, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_vec = np.where(np.isfinite(final_vec), final_vec, 0.0)
    final_vec = np.clip(final_vec, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_vec.size == 0:
        final_vec = np.ones(101, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        lst = [float(v) for v in final_vec]
        if not lst or any((not np.isfinite(v)) or v < 0.0 or v > 1000.0 for v in lst):
            lst = [float(v) for v in np.clip(np.ones(101), 0.0, 1000.0)]
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
            lst = [1.0] * 101
            final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        lst = [1.0] * 101

    return lst
```
